Instead I was crying in my bathroom because I hadn't gone in seven days.\n\nThe cramps from Dulcolax. The bloating from psyllium. The sulfur burps that smelled like rotten eggs and made the woman next to me at coffee visibly recoil. The 3 PM exhaustion that flattened me onto my couch like I was twenty years older than I am. Food in the stomach for twice as long ferments \u2014 that's the sulfur burps. Bacteria breaking down food in a stomach that should have already emptied.\n\nThis is the part where every single item on my $140 receipt failed for the same reason:\n\nFiber doesn't work because fiber adds bulk that needs gut motility to push it through. The problem was upstream of it.\n\nGinger tea didn't work because brewed tea has a tiny fraction of the gingerols you'd need to actually shift gastric motility. The active compound was almost absent in the bottle.\n\nMagnesium oxide didn't work because oxide is the cheapest, least bioavailable form. My body absorbs about 4\u0025 of it. The label said 500mg. I was getting maybe 20mg of usable magnesium per dose.\n\nEight products. Two mechanisms \u2014 bulk-adding or contraction-forcing \u2014 both fighting my medication instead of working with it.\n\nSo what does work?\n\nTwo specific nutrients in two specific forms, addressing the slowed mechanism directly:\n\nMagnesium glycinate \u2014 pulls water into the gut osmotically, without requiring contractions. Works WITH a slowed system instead of fighting it. Bioavailable form your body actually absorbs.\n\nGinger root extract standardized to 5\u0025 gingerols \u2014 a natural prokinetic. Signals gastric emptying without forcing contractions. Doesn't fight the medication. Stops the fermentation that causes the sulfur burps.\n\nPlus methylcobalamin B12 \u2014 the active form, because reduced calorie intake on a GLP-1 has almost certainly depleted your stores, and the cyanocobalamin in cheap multivitamins is the synthetic form that 1 in 3 women can't even convert.\n\nThat's the whole answer. That's why the fiber gummies made my bloating worse \u2014 they were doing exactly what they're designed to do, but in the wrong body for that mechanism.\n\nStimulant laxatives \u2014 Dulcolax, Senna, Bisacodyl, that \"gentle\" laxative tea \u2014 work by forcing intestinal contractions.
